# GetMore OS — Master SEO Keyword Map

> **Dataset Overview**: 6,540 total keywords analyzed from Google Keyword Planner report (`Keyword_Stats_2026-11-09_at_20_03_59.csv`). Zero keywords omitted. Every single keyword is mapped to an assigned existing page or dedicated new pillar / cluster URL.

## 1. Executive Summary & Macro Metrics

- **Total Search Queries Mapped**: 6,540
- **Total Monthly Search Volume Represented**: 3,183,700 searches/month
- **Unique Assigned URLs**: 34 URLs (Covering Core Pillars, Money Pages, Programmatic Industry Verticals & Comparison Hubs)

### Priority Breakdown

| Priority | Definition | Keyword Count | % of Total |
|---|---|---|---|
| **P0** | High Volume (>=5,000) + Low/Med KD (<=60) + Transactional / Commercial | 185 | 2.8% |
| **P1** | High Volume Informational / Navigational + Mid-Volume (500) Transactional / Commercial | 828 | 12.7% |
| **P2** | Long-tail / Low Volume (50 & 0 vol) Supporting Queries | 5,527 | 84.5% |

### Search Intent Distribution

| Search Intent | Keyword Count | % of Total | Strategic Action |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Transactional** | 2,705 | 41.4% | Direct money pages, agency landing pages, conversion funnels, transparent pricing CTA |
| **Commercial** | 1,791 | 27.4% | Software/solution comparisons, platform feature grids, social proof, case studies |
| **Informational** | 1,851 | 28.3% | Comprehensive pillar guides, FAQs, how-to tutorials, framework explanations |
| **Navigational** | 193 | 3.0% | Competitor alternative guides (Salesforce, UiPath, MBB, Bain) redirecting interest to GetMore OS |

## 3. Site Architecture & Technical SEO Audit Findings

### Current Strengths:
- Robust programmatic matrix: 20 distinct industries x 5 service categories (100 landing pages) already established with clean semantic HTML.
- Modern, responsive UI with Tailwind CSS and dark high-conversion branding.
- Fully configured AI crawler allowances in `robots.txt` (GPTBot, ClaudeBot, PerplexityBot, Google-Extended).

### Critical Gaps & Cannibalization Risks Identified:
1. **Duplicate PitchFinder URLs**: `pitchfinder.html` and `pitchfinder/index.html` duplicate content and title tags. Fix: Set canonical of `pitchfinder.html` to `https://getmoreos.in/pitchfinder/`.
2. **Cannibalization in Calling vs Voice Agent Pages**: For each industry, `ai-voice-agent-for-[industry]` and `inbound-outbound-ai-calling-[industry]` target the same search intent. Fix: Cross-link with distinct semantic focus (Inbound Autonomous Reception vs Outbound Cold Reactivation/Lead Qualification) and establish clear parent-child topical links.
3. **Missing Master Pillar Hubs**: Massive high-volume terms (`digital marketing agency` - 50k, `ai automation agency` - 5k, `b2b lead generation` - 5k, `email marketing software` - 50k, `real estate leads` - 5k) currently have NO dedicated master hubs. Fix: Create dedicated pillar pages with internal links out to vertical industry landing pages.
4. **Zero Coverage for Competitor / Alternative Queries**: 700+ high-value keywords search for Salesforce Marketing Cloud, UiPath, Big 4 consulting, and enterprise AI platforms. Fix: Create high-intent comparison and alternative guides under `/blog/` to siphon this organic traffic into GetMore OS service inquiries.
